Let's reconsider continuous variational with no symmetry. We can try solve via optimal control exactly. We need minimize M = sup c, c=f*f. For optimal f, porteglid: At s where c(s)<M, multiplier λ(s)=0; where c=M, λ(s)≥0. Variation: For all t where f(t)>0 (interior [0,1]), ∫ λ(s) f(s-t) ds = const = μ, with λ supported on c=M. If λ constant on a set S (where c=M with positive measure), then (f * 1_S)(t) = μ for t in support. Also c(s)=∫ f(t)f(s-t)dt = M on S.

Suppose S is an interval [α,β] of length L, where c=M. If λ=M? Let's set λ=1 on S. Then f must satisfy ∫_{max(0,t-β)}^{min(1,t-α)} f(u) du = μ (where u=s-t, s∈S => u∈[s-t] => u∈[S-t]). That is the moving average of f over interval S-t is constant. This implies f is constant (if interval length >0 and varies) or piecewise with slope. Specifically, if for all t in [0,1], integral of f over an interval of length L (with varying bounds) is constant, then f is constant almost everywhere in interior. If f constant=1, then c is triangular, and plateau S? For uniform, c(s)=min(s,2-s) not constant interior, so not optimum with plateau constant. Thus λ not constant; it varies where c=M.

Pseudo:
def _local_optimize(f0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    f = normalize(f0).astype(float).copy()
    n = len(f); L=2*n-1
    c = _autocorr(f); maxc = float(c.max()); best_f=f.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=2*n*maxc
    t_end=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    no_accept=0
    total_iter=0
    # phases: list of sigma relative to cmax, e.g. [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.008,0.003,0.001,0.0003,0.0001,3e-5,1e-5,3e-6]
    # But we can adapt: start sigma=0.05 * maxc; if no accept, decrease sigma, occasionally increase for exploration.
    sigma = 0.05*maxc
    min_sigma=1e-8*maxc
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       total_iter+=1
       # recompute c,maxc if f changed (we maintain c)
       if c is None or not finite: recompute
       # update sigma slowly: sigma = max(min_sigma, sigma*0.995 +? ). Or schedule with total_iter.
       # Compute p:
       z = (c - maxc) / (sigma + 1e-300) # <=0
       # prevent underflow: z_clip = z - z.min()? p=exp(z); Since max z=0. p = exp(z); p /= sum
       p = np.exp(np.clip(z, -50, 0.0)); s=p.sum(); if s==0 p uniform? else p/=s
       # For stability, if p effective count too small, maybe use top k uniform.
       g = 2.0 * correlate(p, f, mode='valid')
       # center gradient
       gm = g - g.mean()
       scale = np.max(gm) - np.min(gm)
       if scale < 1e-300: do coordinate/perturb
       # Candidate list exponents: we can use sorted by predicted improvement? Use several.
       best_trial_score = maxc
       best_trial_f = f; best_trial_c = c
       for e in (0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0):
           # mirror
           exponent = -e * gm / scale
           exponent = np.clip(exponent, -50,50)
           f_try = f * np.exp(exponent)
           f_try = normalize(f_try)
           c_try = _autocorr(f_try)
           mc = c_try.max()
           if mc < best_trial_score - 1e-15: best...
           # projected gradient
           f_try2 = _project_simplex(f - e*gm/scale)
           f_try2 = normalize(f_try2)
           c_try2 = _autocorr(f_try2)
           mc2=...
       if best_trial_score < maxc - 1e-15:
           f,best_trial... accept; no_accept=0; # maybe shrink sigma slightly
       else:
           no_accept+=1
           # coordinate polish for a limited amount (0.5s or few passes)
           f2,c2 = _coordinate_polish(f,c, time_min=0.5, rng=rng)
           if c2.max() < maxc - 1e-15: accept
           else:
              if no_accept > 20 or (no_accept % 50==0): perturb; recompute
              # If perturbation accepted? evaluate.
       if best_score update: best_f=f.copy(); best_score=2*n*c.max()
       # update best_score; maybe if stuck long, restart from best with random mixed.
    return best_f,best_score

Coordinate polish details:
def _coordinate_polish(f, c, budget_s, rng):
    f=f.copy(); c=c.copy(); maxc=c.max(); best...
    t_end=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    pass_count=0
    while time.monotonic()<t_end:
        # active set: use top active_tol = 1e-7*maxc? or top 100 relative.
        # We need gradient from some active lags. Use p uniform over top lags where c > maxc - 1e-6*maxc. If too many, top 100; if too few, include top 10.
        active = np.argsort(c)[-top:]; p=zeros; p[active]=1/len; g=2*correlate(p,f,'valid')
        # But active should be centered? Could use hot lags with c > maxc - 1e-5*maxc.
        active = np.where(c >= maxc - 1e-5*maxc)[0]; if len>200: active=top200; if len<5: top5.
        # donors/receivers based on g
        # However moving mass to receiver with low g may reduce active but might increase other lags due to its interactions; max_after catches.
        donor_idx = np.argsort(g)[::-1][:D] (where f>1e-12)
        recv_idx = np.argsort(g)[:R]
        # Also include indices with smallest f? maybe to spread.
        # For speed, candidate pairs from Cartesian. To avoid evaluating same pairs many, could random sample if D*R large.
        best_move = None; best_mc = maxc
        for i in donor_idx:
          for j in recv_idx:
            if i==j: continue
            fi=f[i]
            if fi <= 0: continue
            # t fractions
            for frac in (0.01,0.05,0.15,0.4):
               delta = _transfer_delta(f,c,i,j,frac) # maybe returns max_after
               if maxc_after < best_mc: update
        if best_move: f[i]-=δ; f[j]+=δ; c=best_delta+c; maxc=best_mc; best update; pass_count=0
        else:
           # Try random pairs using top gradients/random: 100 random
           for _ in range(100): i random, j random, frac random.
           if none: break or small perturb
    return

The _transfer_max calc function should avoid Python loops over L. Use vectorized.
def _transfer_max_after(f,c,i,j,frac):
    L=2*len(f)-1
    delta = np.empty(L) maybe zeros each call heavy. Preallocate? Hard because top-level no closures but can create.
    delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    delta[i:i+n] -= f
    delta[j:j+n] += f
    delta *= 2.0*delta_val  # fraction*f[i]
    # quadratic:
    if 2*j < L: delta[2*j] += delta_val*delta_val
    if 2*i < L: delta[2*i] += delta_val*delta_val
    if i+j < L: delta[i+j] -= 2.0*delta_val*delta_val
    total = c + delta
    return float(np.max(total)), delta

Function _local_optimize(f0, budget_s, rng, tag):
    f = normalize(f0)
    n=f.size
    c=_autocorr(f)
    maxc=c.max()
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=2*n*maxc
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    # parameters depend on n
    coord_interval = max(5, n//500?)
    no_accept=0
    sigma_rel=0.08
    min_sigma_rel=1e-9
    iter=0
    last_coord_time=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       iter+=1
       # If no accept long, adjust sigma
       if no_accept > 50:
           sigma_rel = max(min_sigma_rel, sigma_rel*0.5)
           no_accept=0
       # Compute p with softmax
       T = maxc * sigma_rel + 1e-300
       z = (c - maxc) / T
       np.clip(z, -40, 0); p=np.exp(z); s=p.sum(); if s<... p uniform? p/=s
       g = 2.0 * correlate(p, f, mode='valid')
       gm = g - g.mean()
       scale = gm.max() - gm.min()
       accepted=False
       if scale > 1e-18:
           # Try exponents using a list chosen based on scale? 
           # Use 4 candidates: 0.05,0.2,1.0,4.0 (exponent spans)
           best_mc = maxc; best_f_try=None; best_c_try=None
           for e in (0.02,0.1,0.5,2.0,8.0):
              # mirror
              exp_arg = -e * gm / scale
              np.clip(exp_arg,-30,30)
              ft = f * np.exp(exp_arg)
              ft = _normalize(ft)
              ct = _autocorr(ft)
              mc = float(ct.max())
              if mc < best_mc:
                  best_mc=mc; best_f_try=ft; best_c_try=ct
                  # early if very low? no
              # projected gradient maybe only if n large? Let's include for e in subset
              if e in (0.2,1.0,8.0):
                  ft2 = _project_simplex(f - e * gm / scale)
                  ft2 = _normalize(ft2)
                  ct2 = _autocorr(ft2)
                  mc2 = float(ct2.max())
                  if mc2 < best_mc:
                      best_mc=mc2; best_f_try=ft2; best_c_try=ct2
           if best_c_try is not None and best_mc < maxc - 1e-15:
              f=best_f_try; c=best_c_try; maxc=best_mc; accepted=True
               # update best
       if accepted:
           no_accept=0; maybe reduce sigma_rel *=0.97 (sharpen) or keep
           if 2*n*maxc < best_score: update best
       else:
           no_accept += 1
           # Try coordinate polish if not too recently
           if time.monotonic() - last_coord_time > (0.2 if n<5000 else 0.5):
               f2,c2 = _coordinate_polish(f,c, budget_s=0.8, rng=rng, max_passes=3)
               if float(c2.max()) < maxc - 1e-15:
                   f=f2; c=c2; maxc=...; accepted=True; no_accept=0; update best
               last_coord_time=time.monotonic()
           if not accepted and no_accept > 30 or iter % 2000 ==0:
               # perturb
               f = _perturb_best(best_f, f, rng)
               c = _autocorr(f); maxc=c.max()
               no_accept=0
               sigma_rel = min(0.1, sigma_rel*1.5) # broaden
       # Periodic evaluation with evaluate_sequence? maybe every 100000 iterations or 5s
       if iter % 20000 ==0:
           print tag score 2*n*maxc
    return best_f,best_score

Pseudo:
def _gradient_optimize(f,budget,rng,tag=""):
    f=normalize; c=autocorr; maxc; best=copy; best_score; 
    t_end=budget; no_accept=0; next_coord=t_end; sigma_rel=0.05
    while time.monotonic()<t_end:
       p=softmax; g=correlate;
       if acceptable scale: try exponents [0.05,0.2,1,4] mirror only? maybe top two exponents.
       if accept: update; no_accept=0
       else: no_accept++; if no_accept==20 or (now > next_coord): coordinate_polish small 0.5; next_coord=now+3; if no accept after coord: perturb.
    return best.

_project_simplex: Standard algorithm. Need handle v all zeros. Code:
def _project_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v
    u=np.sort(v, axis=0)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho_candidates=np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv-1))[0]
    if len==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    rho=rho_candidates[-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
But if v sum not 1? Project to sum=1; yes.
Normalization after projection not needed but do.

_autocorr: Use np.fft.rfft. Need ensure f real and no NaN. Code:
def _autocorr(f):
    n=f.size
    size=1
    m=2*n-1
    size=1 << (m-1).bit_length() if m>1 else 2
    F=np.fft.rfft(f,size)
    full=np.fft.irfft(F*F,size)
    return full[:m]
Could use float64. For n=1, m=1, size=2? fine.

Let f_rev = f[::-1]. Convolution conv = np.fft.irfft(FFT(p,size)*FFT(f_rev,size), size). We need indices? Earlier h_j = (p * f_rev)[j+n-1]. Convolution length L+n-1=3n-2. size next pow. Slice start n-1 length n. So:
def _grad_weighted(p,f):
    n=f.size; L=p.size
    size=1 << ((L+n-2)-1).bit_length()
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(P*F, size)
    return 2.0*conv[n-1:n-1+n]

Implement _coordinate_polish efficiently:
def _coordinate_polish(f, c, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    n=f.size; L=2*n-1
    f=f.copy(); c=c.copy(); maxc=float(c.max())
    best_f=f.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=2n maxc
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    no_improve=0
    # base c_sum should 1
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and no_improve<20:
       maxc=float(c.max())
       # active set: top 100 or c > maxc - 1e-6*maxc
       thresh = maxc - 1e-5*maxc
       active = np.where(c >= thresh)[0]
       if active.size ==0: active = np.argmax(c) keepdims? 
       elif active.size > 200: active = np.argpartition(c, -200)[-200:]
       # p uniform active
       p=np.zeros(L); p[active]=1.0/active.size
       g=_grad_weighted(p,f)
       # Build donor/receiver candidate pools
       # donors: top g with f > 1e-12
       donor_pool = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
       # receiver_pool: bottom g
       receiver_pool = np.argsort(g)
       D=10 if n>5000 else 20; R=10 if n>5000 else 20
       donors = []
       for idx in donor_pool:
          if f[idx] > 1e-12: donors.append(idx)
          if len(donors)==D: break
       receivers = []
       for idx in receiver_pool:
          if idx not in donors? sets; receivers.append(idx)
          if len(receivers)==R: break
       if len(donors)==0 or len(receivers)==0: break
       # To avoid expensive nested full, sample pairs if D*R large
       best_delta=None; best_mc=maxc
       # Use deterministic loops over donors x receivers, for fracs
       fracs=(0.02,0.08,0.2,0.5)
       # Precompute f slices? For each donor i, linear negative part = -f in slice i:i+n; receiver positive. But each pair differs.
       # We can precompute shifted? no.
       for i in donors:
           fi=f[i]
           if fi <=0: continue
           for j in receivers:
               if i==j: continue
               # unit linear = -f at i slice + f at j slice
               # For each frac, delta_val=fi*frac
               for frac in fracs:
                   dv=fi*frac
                   # allocate delta
                   delta=np.zeros(L)
                   delta[i:i+n] -= f
                   delta[j:j+n] += f
                   delta *= (2.0*dv)
                   quad = dv*dv
                   if 2*j < L: delta[2*j] += quad
                   if 2*i < L: delta[2*i] += quad
                   if i+j < L: delta[i+j] -= 2.0*quad
                   total = c + delta
                   mc = float(np.max(total))
                   if mc < best_mc:
                       best_mc=mc; best_delta=delta; best_i=i; best_j=j; best_dv=dv
       if best_delta is None or not (best_mc < maxc - 1e-15):
           # random pairs
           # maybe 50 random tries
           for _ in range(50):
              i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n)); if i==j continue
              frac=float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.3))
              ... same
           if none: no_improve +=1; break
       else:
           f[best_i]-=best_dv; f[best_j]+=best_dv
           c = c + best_delta
           # ensure nonneg: f=np.maximum(f,0); sum normalize? Transfer preserves sum, but due rounding. If clipping, renormalize.
           if f.min()<0: f=np.maximum(f,0); f/=f.sum(); recompute c? If min tiny, okay.
           maxc=best_mc
           if 2n maxc < best_score: update
           no_improve=0
    return best_f,best_score,best_c?

def _finalize(f):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    f = np.clip(f, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = f.sum()

    # maybe scale sum to 1? scale invariance, but ensure sum not too small.
    f = f/s
    # To avoid underflow? okay.
    # Use evaluate_sequence to confirm
    return [float(x) for x in f]

Detailed gradient_optimize implementation:
```
def _gradient_optimize(f0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    if budget_s <= 0: return f0, _fast_score(f0)
    n = len(f0)
    f = _normalize(f0)
    c = _autocorr(f)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    best_f = f.copy(); best_score=2*n*maxc
    deadline = time.monotonic()+budget_s
    sigma_rel = 0.08
    no_accept = 0
    last_coord = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if maxc <= 0: break
        T = maxc * sigma_rel + 1e-300
        z = (c - maxc) / T
        np.clip(z, -45.0, 0.0, out=z)
        p = np.exp(z)
        sp = p.sum()
        if sp < 1e-300:
            p.fill(1.0/(2*n-1))
        else:
            p /= sp
        g = _weighted_gradient(p, f)
        gm = g - g.mean()
        scale = float(gm.max() - gm.min())
        accepted = False
        if scale > 1e-300:
            best_mc = maxc; best_ft=None; best_ct=None
            # exponents: use maybe based on no_accept: if large, try small first? We'll test list.
            exps = (0.02, 0.1, 0.4, 1.5, 6.0)
            for e in exps:
                arg = -e * (gm / scale)
                # clip
                np.clip(arg, -35.0, 35.0, out=arg)
                ft = f * np.exp(arg)
                ft = _normalize(ft)
                ct = _autocorr(ft)
                mc = float(ct.max())
                if mc < best_mc:
                    best_mc = mc; best_ft = ft; best_ct = ct
                # Projected maybe for e 0.4,1.5
                if e == 0.4 or e == 1.5:
                    v = f - e * (gm / scale)
                    ft2 = _project_simplex(v)
                    ft2 = _normalize(ft2)
                    ct2 = _autocorr(ft2)
                    mc2 = float(ct2.max())
                    if mc2 < best_mc:
                        best_mc=mc2; best_ft=ft2; best_ct=ct2
            if best_ct is not None and best_mc < maxc - 1e-15:
                f = best_ft; c = best_ct; maxc = best_mc; accepted=True
        if accepted:
            no_accept = 0
            if 2*n*maxc < best_score:
                best_score = 2*n*maxc; best_f = f.copy()
            # gradually shorten sigma
            if no_accept <= 10: # no, accepted
                sigma_rel = max(1e-10, sigma_rel * 0.985)
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            # coordinate polish if frequent
            now=time.monotonic()
            if now - last_coord > 1.5 or no_accept > 30:
                f2, sc2, c2 = _coordinate_polish(f, c, budget_s=0.7, rng=rng)
                if sc2 < 2*n*maxc - 1e-12:
                    f = _normalize(f2); c = c2; maxc = float(c.max()); accepted=True
                    no_accept=0
                    if sc2 < best_score:
                        best_score=sc2; best_f=f.copy()
                else:
                    # perturb after many
                    if no_accept > 80 or it % 4000 == 0:
                        f = _perturb(best_f, f, rng)
                        f = _normalize(f)
                        c = _autocorr(f); maxc=float(c.max())
                        sigma_rel = min(0.15, sigma_rel * 2.0)
                        no_accept = 0
                last_coord = time.monotonic()
        # Periodic print
        if it % 50000 == 0:
            print(tag, "score", best_score, "maxc", maxc, "sigma", sigma_rel)
    if 2*n*c.max() < best_score: update
    return best_f, best_score
```
Potential issue: If accepted update c=best_ct but c might not exactly correspond after _normalize? We computed from ft. Good.

_perturb:
```
def _perturb(best_f, current_f, rng):
    n=len(best_f)
    # mixture
    if rng.random() <0.5:
       sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 1.0))
       noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
       f = best_f * np.exp(sigma*noise)
    else:
       alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.6))
       noise = rng.random(n); noise/=noise.sum()
       f = (1-alpha)*best_f + alpha*noise
    return _normalize(f)
```

Spectral flatten algorithm:
```
def _spectral_seed(f, target_c=None, iterations=2000, rng):
   n=len(f); L=2n-1; N=pow2>=L
   f=normalize; A=fft(f,N)
   if target_c is None:
       # ideal target: current c but peak flattened: t = c.copy(); t[t>0.95*max] = 0.95*max; t/=sum
   T=fft(target_c,N)
   for it:
       # multiplicative spectral update
       # compute log of A, T. Need handle zeros. Use direct formula: A_new = A * (T / (A*A))^{alpha/2}
       # ratio = T / (A*A)
       # to update phase/mag: A_new = A * np.exp(0.5*alpha*np.log(ratio + 1e-30))
       # But if A=0, set A=small with target phase.
       mask = np.abs(A)>1e-300
       ratio = np.zeros_like(A)
       ratio[mask] = T[mask] / (A[mask]*A[mask])
       # log ratio may have arbitrary branch; use np.log
       A[mask] = A[mask] * np.exp(0.5*alpha*np.log(ratio[mask]))
       # For unmasked, set A = sqrt(T)
       A[~mask] = np.sqrt(T[~mask])
       # Inverse
       f_new = np.fft.irfft(A, N)[:n]
       f_new = np.maximum(f_new,0)
       f_new = normalize
   return f
```
This tries to minimize (A²-T). But if T coefficients target not a square, no exact. We can choose T = current c (then update should not change except numerical); positivity projection changes A, T? If T=current c and f updated, maybe just smoothing? If T=flat, explore. Could be a good global method. But log of complex ratio with zeros phase issues; alpha small 0.1.

```
def _downsample_f(f, K):
   n=len(f); x0=np.linspace(0,n-1,n); x1=np.linspace(0,n-1,K)
   return _normalize(np.interp(x1,x0,f))  # Actually np.interp expects xp increasing; x1. Good.
```

Let's implement each.

_normalize:
```
def _normalize(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    v[~np.isfinite(v)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(v, 0.0, out=v)
    s = float(v.sum())
    n = v.size
    if s <= 1e-300 or n == 0:
        return np.ones(max(1,n), dtype=np.float64) / max(1,n)
    v /= s
    # cap? if any >1000? not for sum1. But if n=0 handled.
    return v
```
If n large, uniform.

_pow2:
```
def _next_power_of_two(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
For m=2n-1. If m=1, size=1? rfft length 1 okay? But for FFT, size 1 maybe. Use max(2,...). Simpler:
```
def _fft_size(m):
    if m <= 1: return 2
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
Ensure at least 2. For m=1 (n=1), size2.

_autocorr:
```
def _autocorr(f):
    n=f.size
    m=2*n-1
    size=_fft_size(m)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f, size)
    full=np.fft.irfft(F*F, size)
    return full[:m]
```
For complex? F*F not abs, correct square. irfft returns real.

_fast_score:
```
def _fast_score(f):
    f = _normalize(f)
    c=_autocorr(f)
    return float(2.0*len(f)*c.max())
```
But normalizing each time copies; for seeds fine. For internal, no.

_project_simplex:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v.copy()
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    w=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u * w > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond): return np.ones(n)/n
    rho=int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    out=v-theta
    np.maximum(out,0.0,out)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
    # numerical: may sum slightly !=1
    out/=s
    return out
```
Good.

_weighted_gradient:
```
def _weighted_gradient(p, f):
    n=f.size
    L=p.size
    m=L+n-1
    size=_fft_size(m)
    # p length L, f rev length n
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(P*F, size)
    return 2.0*conv[n-1:n+n-1]
```
Check slice: n-1 to n-1+n = 2n-2 length n? For n=2 slice 1:3 length2 yes. `n-1:n+n-1` = 1:3. Good.
If p length not 2n-1? We call with L. Good.

_coordinate_polish:
Need manage time; high-level:
```
def _coordinate_polish(f0, c0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    n = len(f0)
    if n == 0 or budget_s <=0: return f0, 0, c0
    f = _normalize(f0)
    c = np.asarray(c0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    # if c length not 2n-1 or NaN, recompute
    if c.size != 2*n-1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        c = _autocorr(f)
    L=2*n-1
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_mc = maxc
    best_score = 2.0*n*maxc
    deadline = time.monotonic() + budget_s
    delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    total = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    no_improve=0
    # choose D,R
    if n > 20000: D=6; R=6
    elif n > 5000: D=10; R=10
    else: D=16; R=16
    fracs = (0.02, 0.08, 0.2, 0.5)
    # active top K
    activeK = min(L, max(20, 3*max(D,R)))? For gradient choose maybe 80.
    activeK = min(L, 100 if n>5000 else 40)
    move_count=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < 25:
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        # active: use softmax? simpler top activeK
        # For small n, top activeK many; for large, 80.
        if activeK >= L:
            active = np.arange(L)
        else:
            active = np.argpartition(c, L-activeK)[L-activeK:]  # top activeK unsorted
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[active] = 1.0/active.size
        g = _weighted_gradient(p,f)
        # donors/receivers
        donor_order = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
        recv_order = np.argsort(g)
        donors=[]
        for idx in donor_order:
            if f[idx] > 1e-12:
                donors.append(int(idx))
                if len(donors) >= D: break
        receivers=[]
        # avoid donors? Use set maybe.
        donor_set = set(donors)
        for idx in recv_order:
            idx=int(idx)
            if idx not in donor_set:
                receivers.append(idx)
                if len(receivers) >= R: break
        if not donors or not receivers:
            no_improve += 1
            # maybe random fallback
            ...
            continue
        best_mc = maxc
        best_move=None
        # limit number candidate pairs for time. If D*R*fracs big, sample.
        # Use all if <= 2000 else random sample 800
        pairs = [(i,j) for i in donors for j in receivers] ??? list comprehension no lambda okay but memory small.
        # Could nested loops.
        for i in donors:
            fi=f[i]
            if fi <=0: continue
            for j in receivers:
                if i==j: continue
                for frac in fracs:
                    dv=fi*frac
                    delta.fill(0.0)
                    delta[i:i+n] -= f
                    delta[j:j+n] += f
                    delta *= (2.0*dv)
                    q=dv*dv
                    if 2*j < L: delta[2*j] += q
                    if 2*i < L: delta[2*i] += q
                    if i+j < L: delta[i+j] -= 2.0*q
                    np.add(c, delta, out=total)
                    mc=float(np.max(total))
                    if mc < best_mc:
                        best_mc = mc
                        best_move=(i,j,dv,delta.copy())
        if best_move is None or best_mc >= maxc - 1e-15:
            # random try
            for _ in range(30):
                i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
                if i==j or f[i]<=0: continue
                frac=float(rng.uniform(0.01,0.35))
                dv=f[i]*frac
                ...
            if best_move is None or ...:
                no_improve += 1
                # small perturb? maybe break if budget enough? continue
                # Could mix f with best_f slightly
                if no_improve > 5:
                    f = (1-0.05)*f + 0.05*best_f? best_f same.
                continue
        # accept
        i,j,dv,d_saved = best_move
        f[i] -= dv; f[j] += dv
        c = c + d_saved  # this alloc; ok
        maxc = best_mc
        move_count += 1
        if 2*n*maxc < best_score:
            best_score = 2*n*maxc; best_f = f.copy()
        no_improve=0
        if move_count % 50 == 0:
            # prevent drift
            f = _normalize(f)
            c = _autocorr(f)
            maxc = float(c.max())
            if 2*n*maxc < best_score:
                best_score=2*n*maxc; best_f=f.copy()
    # final recompute best
    f = _normalize(f); c=_autocorr(f); mc=float(c.max())
    if 2*n*mc <= best_score:
        return f, 2*n*mc, c
    else:
        bf=_normalize(best_f); bc=_autocorr(bf); bmc=float(bc.max()); return bf, 2*n*bmc, bc
```

Implementation details:
```
def _gradient_optimize(f0,budget_s,rng,tag=""):
    if budget_s <=0:
       f=_normalize(f0); return f,_fast_score(f)
    f=_normalize(f0)
    c=_autocorr(f)
    maxc=float(c.max()); n=f.size
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=2*n*maxc
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    sigma_rel=0.08
    no_accept=0
    last_coord=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    coord_budget = 0.3 if n<5000 else 0.6
    min_sigma=1e-10
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if maxc <= 0: break
        T = maxc * sigma_rel + 1e-300
        z = (c - maxc) / T
        np.clip(z, -45.0, 0.0, out=z)
        p = np.exp(z)
        s = p.sum()
        if s < 1e-300:
            p = np.full(2*n-1, 1.0/(2*n-1))
        else:
            p /= s
        g = _weighted_gradient(p, f)
        gm = g - g.mean()
        scale = float(gm.max() - gm.min())
        accepted=False
        if scale > 1e-300:
            best_mc=maxc; best_ft=None; best_ct=None
            # adapt exponents: if no_accept large, use smaller
            if no_accept > 30:
                exps=(0.01,0.05,0.2,0.8)
            else:
                exps=(0.05,0.2,0.8,3.0,10.0)
            for e in exps:
                arg = -e * (gm / scale)
                np.clip(arg, -35.0, 35.0, out=arg)
                ft = f * np.exp(arg)
                ft = _normalize(ft)
                ct = _autocorr(ft)
                mc = float(ct.max())
                if mc < best_mc:
                    best_mc=mc; best_ft=ft; best_ct=ct
                # project for middle e
                if e in (0.8, 3.0):
                    v = f - e * (gm / scale)
                    ft2 = _project_simplex(v)
                    ft2 = _normalize(ft2)
                    ct2 = _autocorr(ft2)
                    mc2 = float(ct2.max())
                    if mc2 < best_mc:
                        best_mc=mc2; best_ft=ft2; best_ct=ct2
            if best_ct is not None and best_mc < maxc - 1e-15:
                f=best_ft; c=best_ct; maxc=best_mc; accepted=True
        if accepted:
            no_accept=0
            cur_score=2*n*maxc
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best_score=cur_score; best_f=f.copy()
            sigma_rel = max(min_sigma, sigma_rel*0.99)
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            now=time.monotonic()
            if now - last_coord > 0.8 or no_accept > 20:
                fp, sc, cp = _coordinate_polish(f, c, budget_s=coord_budget, rng=rng)
                if sc < 2*n*maxc - 1e-12:
                    f = _normalize(fp); c = cp; maxc = float(c.max())
                    accepted = True
                    no_accept = 0
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc; best_f = f.copy()
                else:
                    if no_accept > 60 or (it % 6000 == 0):
                        f = _perturb(best_f, f, rng)
                        c = _autocorr(f); maxc = float(c.max())
                        sigma_rel = min(0.2, sigma_rel * 2.0 + 1e-4)
                        no_accept = 0
                last_coord = time.monotonic()
            else:
                sigma_rel = max(min_sigma, sigma_rel * 0.995) # maybe
        if it % 20000 == 0:
            print(tag, "iter", it, "best", best_score, "cur", 2*n*maxc)
    cur_score=2*n*float(c.max())
    if cur_score < best_score:
       best_score=cur_score; best_f=f.copy()
    return best_f, best_score
```
When coordinate returns `cp` from current/best, if `sc` is exact and < current, we use f=fp, c=cp. If no improve, we discard coordinate f (maybe it made no score improvement but could have structural change? no). Good.

If accepted from coordinate, we don't update c if coordinate best_f was not same as current? _coordinate_polish returns best current or best_f; we set f to it. Good.

```
def _downsample_to(f,K):
   n=f.size
   if K >= n: return _normalize(f)
   xf=np.linspace(0,n-1,n); xk=np.linspace(0,n-1,K)
   return _normalize(np.interp(xk, xf, f))
```

Implement `_smooth_with_window(f, win)`:
```
def _smooth_with_window(f, win):
    n=f.size
    if n <= 1: return f.copy()
    if win < 2: win=2
    if win > n: win = n // 2
    kernel = np.ones(win, dtype=np.float64) / float(win)
    out = np.convolve(f, kernel, mode='same')
    return _normalize(out)
```

Implement `_interp_to_length(f, n)`:
```
def _interp_to_length(f,n):
    m=f.size
    if m==n: return _normalize(f)
    if n==1: return _normalize(np.array([f.sum()]))
    if m==1: return _normalize(np.full(n,f[0]))
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0, m-1, m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0, n-1, n)
    return _normalize(np.interp(x_new, x_old, f))
```

We can top-level `_evaluate_transfer(c, f, i, j, dv, L, n, delta, total)` returning mc and maybe not copy. But to avoid passing many args? Top-level function okay. But it would allocate/copy? We can implement:
```
def _transfer_max(c, f, i, j, dv, delta, total):
    n=f.size; L=c.size
    delta.fill(0.0)
    delta[i:i+n] -= f
    delta[j:j+n] += f
    delta *= 2.0*dv
    q=dv*dv
    if 2*j < L: delta[2*j] += q
    if 2*i < L: delta[2*i] += q
    if i+j < L: delta[i+j] -= 2.0*q
    np.add(c, delta, out=total)
    return float(np.max(total))
```
Then in loops, if mc<best, best_delta = delta.copy(). This top-level no lambda. Good.

Implement `_try_add_seed`:
```
def _try_add_seed(seeds, name, f, n):
    f = _interp_to_length(f, n)
    try:
        sc = _fast_score(f)
    except Exception:
        return
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        seeds.append((float(sc), len(seeds), str(name), f))
```

In `_build_seeds` with random fourier, need `rng` not None. Use `if rng is not None`.

Code for random seeds:
```
def _random_fourier_seed(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = np.zeros(n)
    u += rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) * (x - 0.5)
    for m in (1,2,3,5):
        u += 0.5 * rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) * np.cos(2.0*np.pi*m*x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi))
    u += 0.5 * rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0) * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
    f = np.exp(0.7 * u)
    return _normalize(f)
```
Top-level. Beta seed top-level.
```
def _beta_seed(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    a = float(rng.uniform(0.2,3.0)); b = float(rng.uniform(0.2,3.0))
    f = (x + 0.005)**a * (1.0-x + 0.005)**b
    return _normalize(f)
```
Could be negative? x+ positive.

Maybe include `f0 ** gamma` with f0 zeros and gamma<1: zeros^frac=0, okay. If gamma negative? no.

```python
"""
Step-function optimizer for
    score(a) = 2*n*max_k a_k*a_{*}a_k / (sum a)^2.

The optimizer works on the simplex sum(a)=1 and uses:
  - FFT-based evaluation of A(x)^2 coefficients,
  - annealed soft/active-set gradients in log-domain and projected Euclidean steps,
  - exact two-coordinate transfer polish,
  - coarse-dimension restarts and several analytic/random starting points.

All helpers are top-level. No filesystem or network access is used.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027


def _normalize(v):
    """Project to non-negative float vector of sum 1."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    v[~np.isfinite(v)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(v, 0.0, out=v)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(v.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(v.size)
    v /= s
    return v


def _fft_size(m):
    """Smallest power of two at least m and at least 2."""
    if m <= 2:
        return 2
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _autocorr(f):
    """Full linear convolution f*f via FFT. f is assumed sum-normalized."""
    n = f.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = _fft_size(m)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f, size)
    full = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)
    return full[:m]


def _fast_score(f):
    """Fast score for a normalized non-negative vector."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    if f.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = _autocorr(f)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * f.size * c.max())


def _project_simplex(v):
    """Euclidean projection onto x >= 0, sum x = 1."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v.copy()
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1.0)
    out = v - theta
    np.maximum(out, 0.0, out)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    out /= s
    return out


def _weighted_gradient(p, f):
    """
    Gradient-like vector g_j = 2 * sum_k p_k f_{k-j}
    where p has length 2n-1 and f has length n.
    """
    n = f.size
    L = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = L + n - 1
    size = _fft_size(m)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(P * F, size)
    return 2.0 * conv[n - 1:n + n - 1]


def _interp_to_length(f, n):
    """Linearly resample f to length n and normalize."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    m = f.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == n:
        return f
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 1:
        return _normalize(np.full(n, f[0]))
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1, m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1, n, dtype=np.float64)
    return _normalize(np.interp(x_new, x_old, f))


def _smooth_with_window(f, win):
    """Uniform moving-average smoothing."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = f.size
    if n <= 2:
        return f
    win = int(win)
    if win < 3:
        win = 3
    if win > n:
        win = max(3, n // 2)
    kernel = np.ones(win, dtype=np.float64) / float(win)
    out = np.convolve(f, kernel, mode="same")
    return _normalize(out)


def _beta_seed(n, rng):
    """Randomly asymmetric beta-shaped seed."""
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
    b = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
    f = (x + 0.005) ** a * (1.0 - x + 0.005) ** b
    return _normalize(f)


def _fourier_seed(n, rng):
    """Smooth low-frequency perturbation of uniform."""
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    u += float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)) * (x - 0.5)
    for m in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8):
        u += 0.40 * float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)) * np.cos(
            2.0 * np.pi * m * x + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        )
    u += 0.40 * float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)) * np.sin(
        2.0 * np.pi * x + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    )
    # Keep positive without extreme peaks.
    f = np.exp(0.65 * u)
    return _normalize(f)


def _try_add_seed(seeds, name, f, n):
    """Evaluate and append a candidate seed if finite."""
    f = _interp_to_length(f, n)
    try:
        sc = _fast_score(f)
    except Exception:
        return
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        # Unique second entry avoids comparing arrays during sort.
        seeds.append((float(sc), len(seeds), str(name), f))


def _transfer_max(c, f, i, j, dv, delta, total):
    """
    Return max_k c'_k for the two-coordinate transfer
        f[i] -= dv, f[j] += dv
    without modifying f/c. Pre-allocated delta,total are used.
    """
    n = f.size
    L = c.size
    delta.fill(0.0)
    delta[i:i + n] -= f
    delta[j:j + n] += f
    delta *= (2.0 * dv)
    q = dv * dv
    # i,j in [0,n-1] imply all these indices are valid in length 2n-1.
    delta[2 * j] += q
    delta[2 * i] += q
    delta[i + j] -= 2.0 * q
    np.add(c, delta, out=total)
    return float(np.max(total))


def _coordinate_polish(f0, c0, budget_s, rng):
    """
    Local search by exact single mass transfers.
    Returns (f, score, c) for the best found state.
    """
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n < 2 or budget_s <= 0.0:
        c = _autocorr(f)
        return f, float(2.0 * n * c.max()), c

    L = 2 * n - 1
    try:
        c = np.asarray(c0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    except Exception:
        c = _autocorr(f)
    if c.size != L or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        c = _autocorr(f)

    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_mc = maxc
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget_s)
    delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    total = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    no_improve = 0
    move_count = 0

    if n > 20000:
        D = 6
        R = 6
        activeK = 120
    elif n > 5000:
        D = 10
        R = 10
        activeK = 180
    else:
        D = 14
        R = 14
        activeK = max(24, min(L, n // 8))

    activeK = int(min(L, max(20, activeK)))
    fracs = (0.02, 0.08, 0.20, 0.50)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < 12:
        maxc = float(np.max(c))

        # Active lags used to choose donors/receivers.
        if activeK >= L:
            active = np.arange(L)
        else:
            active = np.argpartition(c, L - activeK)[L - activeK:]
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        if active.size:
            p[active] = 1.0 / float(active.size)
            g = _weighted_gradient(p, f)
        else:
            g = _weighted_gradient(p, f)

        donor_order = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
        recv_order = np.argsort(g)

        donors = []
        for idx in donor_order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if f[idx] > 1e-12:
                donors.append(idx)
                if len(donors) >= D:
                    break

        donor_set = set(donors)
        receivers = []
        for idx in recv_order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if idx not in donor_set:
                receivers.append(idx)
                if len(receivers) >= R:
                    break

        if not donors or not receivers:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 5:
                break
            continue

        donor_units = []
        for i in donors:
            u = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            u[i:i + n] = -f
            donor_units.append((i, u))

        recv_units = []
        for j in receivers:
            u = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            u[j:j + n] = f
            recv_units.append((j, u))

        best_mc = maxc
        best_move = None

        # Deterministic candidate transfers guided by active gradient.
        for i, ui in donor_units:
            fi = float(f[i])
            if fi <= 1e-12:
                continue
            for j, uj in recv_units:
                if i == j:
                    continue
                for frac in fracs:
                    dv = fi * frac
                    if dv <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    np.add(ui, uj, out=delta)
                    delta *= (2.0 * dv)
                    q = dv * dv
                    delta[2 * j] += q
                    delta[2 * i] += q
                    delta[i + j] -= 2.0 * q
                    np.add(c, delta, out=total)
                    mc = float(np.max(total))
                    if mc < best_mc:
                        best_mc = mc
                        best_move = (i, j, dv, delta.copy())

        # Random fallback transfers.
        if best_move is None or best_mc >= maxc - 1e-18:
            for _ in range(25):
                i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                if i == j or f[i] <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                frac = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.35))
                dv = float(f[i]) * frac
                mc = _transfer_max(c, f, i, j, dv, delta, total)
                if mc < best_mc:
                    best_mc = mc
                    best_move = (i, j, dv, delta.copy())

        if best_move is None or best_mc >= maxc - 1e-18:
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        i, j, dv, d_saved = best_move
        f[i] -= dv
        f[j] += dv
        c = c + d_saved
        move_count += 1
        no_improve = 0
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * best_mc)
        if cur_score < best_score:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_f = f.copy()

        # Avoid numerical drift from incremental updates.
        if move_count % 50 == 0:
            f = _normalize(f)
            c = _autocorr(f)
            maxc = float(np.max(c))
            cur_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_f = f.copy()

    f = _normalize(f)
    c = _autocorr(f)
    if c.size:
        mc = float(c.max())
        sc = float(2.0 * n * mc)
        if sc <= best_score:
            return f, sc, c

    bf = _normalize(best_f)
    bc = _autocorr(bf)
    bmc = float(bc.max()) if bc.size else 0.0
    return bf, float(2.0 * n * bmc), bc


def _perturb(best_f, current_f, rng):
    """Perturb from the best state, either multiplicatively or by mixing."""
    best_f = _normalize(best_f)
    current_f = _normalize(current_f)
    n = best_f.size
    r = float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.55:
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 1.0))
        z = rng.standard_normal(n)
        f = best_f * np.exp(sigma * z)
    elif r < 0.85:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.45))
        noise = rng.random(n)
        s = noise.sum()
        if s >= 1e-300:
            noise /= s
        f = (1.0 - alpha) * best_f + alpha * noise
    else:
        # Smooth best then roughen.
        f = _smooth_with_window(best_f, max(3, n // 200))
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.5))
        f = f * np.exp(sigma * rng.standard_normal(n))
    return _normalize(f)


def _gradient_optimize(f0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    """
    Annealed gradient/line-search optimizer in log domain, with periodic
    exact coordinate polish and perturbations.
    """
    f0 = _normalize(f0)
    n = f0.size
    if n == 0:
        return f0, np.inf
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        c = _autocorr(f0)
        return f0, float(2.0 * n * c.max())

    f = f0
    c = _autocorr(f)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget_s)
    sigma_rel = 0.08
    min_sigma_rel = 1e-10
    no_accept = 0
    it = 0
    last_coord = time.monotonic()

    if n < 2000:
        coord_budget = 0.25
        coord_interval = 0.40
    elif n < 10000:
        coord_budget = 0.45
        coord_interval = 0.60
    else:
        coord_budget = 0.70
        coord_interval = 0.80

    print(f"[{tag}] start score={best_score:.6f}")

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        if maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        # Softmax weights over lags, temperature relative to current peak.
        T = maxc * sigma_rel + 1e-300
        z = (c - maxc) / T
        np.clip(z, -45.0, 0.0, out=z)
        p = np.exp(z)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps < 1e-300:
            p.fill(1.0 / float(c.size))
        else:
            p /= ps

        g = _weighted_gradient(p, f)
        gm = g - float(g.mean())
        scale = float(gm.max() - gm.min())

        accepted = False
        if scale > 1e-300:
            best_mc = maxc
            best_ft = None
            best_ct = None

            if no_accept > 30:
                exps = (0.005, 0.02, 0.1, 0.5)
            else:
                exps = (0.02, 0.1, 1.0, 5.0)

            for e in exps:
                # Log-domain mirror step.
                arg = -e * (gm / scale)
                np.clip(arg, -35.0, 35.0, out=arg)
                ft = f * np.exp(arg)
                ft = _normalize(ft)
                ct = _autocorr(ft)
                mc = float(ct.max())
                if mc < best_mc:
                    best_mc = mc
                    best_ft = ft
                    best_ct = ct

                # Projected Euclidean step for a couple of middle/large scales.
                if e in (0.1, 1.0):
                    v = f - e * (gm / scale)
                    ft2 = _project_simplex(v)
                    ft2 = _normalize(ft2)
                    ct2 = _autocorr(ft2)
                    mc2 = float(ct2.max())
                    if mc2 < best_mc:
                        best_mc = mc2
                        best_ft = ft2
                        best_ct = ct2

            if best_ct is not None and best_mc < maxc - 1e-18:
                f = best_ft
                c = best_ct
                maxc = best_mc
                accepted = True

        if accepted:
            no_accept = 0
            cur_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_f = f.copy()
                if best_score < TARGET_SCORE:
                    print(f"[{tag}] new best below target: {best_score:.6f}")
            sigma_rel = max(min_sigma_rel, sigma_rel * 0.99)
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now - last_coord > coord_interval or no_accept > 20:
                fp, sc, cp = _coordinate_polish(f, c, coord_budget, rng)
                if sc < float(2.0 * n * maxc) - 1e-12:
                    f = _normalize(fp)
                    c = cp
                    maxc = float(c.max())
                    accepted = True
                    no_accept = 0
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_f = f.copy()
                        if best_score < TARGET_SCORE:
                            print(f"[{tag}] coordinate improved below target: {best_score:.6f}")
                else:
                    if no_accept > 55 or (it % 6000 == 0):
                        f = _perturb(best_f, f, rng)
                        c = _autocorr(f)
                        maxc = float(c.max())
                        sigma_rel = min(0.25, sigma_rel * 2.0 + 1e-4)
                        no_accept = 0
                last_coord = time.monotonic()
            else:
                sigma_rel = max(min_sigma_rel, sigma_rel * 0.995)

        if it % 20000 == 0:
            cur_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
            print(f"[{tag}] iter={it} best={best_score:.6f} cur={cur_score:.6f} sigma={sigma_rel:.3e}")

    cur_score = float(2.0 * n * float(c.max()))
    if cur_score < best_score:
        best_score = cur_score
        best_f = f.copy()

    print(f"[{tag}] end best={best_score:.6f}")
    return _normalize(best_f), float(best_score)


def _downsample_to(f, K):
    """Downsample by linear interpolation to K grid points."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = f.size
    if K <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if K >= n:
        return f
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, K - 1, K, dtype=np.float64)
    return _normalize(np.interp(x_new, x_old, f))


def _upsample_to(f, n):
    """Upsample to length n by linear interpolation."""
    return _interp_to_length(f, n)


def _build_seed_pool(f0, n, rng):
    """Create and sort a pool of diverse starting sequences."""
    seeds = []
    f0 = _interp_to_length(f0, n)

    _try_add_seed(seeds, "given", f0, n)
    _try_add_seed(seeds, "reverse", f0[::-1], n)

    for win in (5, 11, 31, 101):
        _try_add_seed(seeds, f"smooth{win}", _smooth_with_window(f0, win), n)

    for gamma in (0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.5):
        g = float(gamma)
        base = f0 + 1e-12
        _try_add_seed(seeds, f"gamma{gamma}", np.power(base, g), n)

    _try_add_seed(seeds, "uniform", np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)

    for i in range(12):
        _try_add_seed(seeds, f"fourier{i}", _fourier_seed(n, rng), n)
    for i in range(8):
        _try_add_seed(seeds, f"beta{i}", _beta_seed(n, rng), n)

    # Coarse-to-fine smoothings of the given construction.
    if n > 200:
        for K in (128, 256, 512, 1024):
            if K < n:
                coarse = _downsample_to(f0, K)
                _try_add_seed(seeds, f"coarse{k if False else K}", _upsample_to(coarse, n), n)

    if not seeds:
        f = np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        seeds.append((2.0, 0, "fallback uniform", _normalize(f)))

    seeds.sort()
    return seeds


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing
    evaluate_sequence. Returns a Python list of floats.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    total_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(120.0, float(budget_s)) - 25.0

    # Load the provided construction if available.
    try:
        f0 = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        f0 = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)

    if f0.size == 0:
        f0 = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)
    f0 = np.nan_to_num(f0, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.clip(f0, 0.0, 1000.0, out=f0)
    n = int(f0.size)
    if n < 3:
        f0 = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        n = f0.size
    f0 = _normalize(f0)

    best_f = f0.copy()
    best_c = _autocorr(best_f)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_c.max()) if best_c.size else np.inf

    try:
        seed_pool = _build_seed_pool(f0, n, rng)
        if not seed_pool:
            seed_pool = [(best_score, 0, "given", best_f)]

        # Optional coarse exploration: optimize a low-dimensional garned state,
        # then lift it back to full resolution.
        now = time.monotonic()
        if total_deadline - now > 1100.0 and n > 256:
            coarse_time = 35.0
            coarse_start_pool = [seed_pool[0][3]]
            coarse_start_pool.append(_fourier_seed(512, rng))
            coarseKs = [512, 256]

            for ci in range(2):
                if total_deadline - time.monotonic() < coarse_time + 60.0:
                    break
                K = coarseKs[ci]
                source = coarse_start_pool[ci] if ci < len(coarse_start_pool) else coarse_start_pool[0]
                source = _downsample_to(source, K)
                cf, cs = _gradient_optimize(source, coarse_time, rng, f"coarse{K}")
                fin = _upsample_to(cf, n)
                _try_add_seed(seed_pool, f"coarse_lift_{K}", fin, n)

            seed_pool.sort()

        # Reserve final exact coordinate-polish time.
        now = time.monotonic()
        if total_deadline - now > 350.0:
            final_reserve = min(330.0, max(60.0, 0.13 * (total_deadline - now)))
        else:
            final_reserve = max(0.0, total_deadline - now - 30.0)

        main_deadline = total_deadline - final_reserve
        main_budget = max(1.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic())

        # Optimize the best few distinctly different full-length starts.
        top = seed_pool[:4] if len(seed_pool) >= 4 else seed_pool
        # Avoid spending too much on almost-identical top seeds.
        weights = [0.52, 0.28, 0.15, 0.05][:len(top)]
        t_cursor = time.monotonic()
        main_start = t_cursor

        for idx, item in enumerate(top):
            sc, _id, name, f = item
            if time.monotonic() >= main_deadline - 15.0:
                break
            # Reallocate remaining main budget among remaining seeds smoothly.
            remain_to_idx = main_deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0
            if remain_to_idx <= 30.0:
                break
            frac = weights[idx] / sum(weights[idx:]) if idx < len(weights) else 0.0
            alloc = max(20.0, min(remain_to_idx - 5.0, 0.90 * main_budget * frac))
            if alloc > max(30.0, main_budget * 0.55) and idx == 0:
                # First seed gets most, but not all, of the main budget.
                alloc = min(alloc, main_budget * 0.58)
            print(f"\n=== full optimize #{idx+1} seed={name} alloc={alloc:.1f}s current_best={best_score:.6f} ===")
            f_opt, sc_opt = _gradient_optimize(f, alloc, rng, f"full{name}")
            if sc_opt < best_score:
                best_score = sc_opt
                best_f = f_opt.copy()
                best_c = _autocorr(best_f)

        # Final dedicated coordinate polish.
        now = time.monotonic()
        final_time = max(0.0, total_deadline - now - 20.0)
        if final_time > 25.0:
            print(f"\n=== final coordinate polish budget={final_time:.1f}s ===")
            bf, bs, bc = _coordinate_polish(best_f, _autocorr(best_f), final_time, rng)
            if bs < best_score:
                best_score = bs
                best_f = bf.copy()
                best_c = bc

        # If time remains, one more mixed pass from the current best.
        now = time.monotonic()
        extra = max(0.0, total_deadline - now - 15.0)
        if extra > 150.0:
            grad_extra = min(180.0, 0.55 * extra)
            print(f"\n=== final gradient pass budget={grad_extra:.1f}s ===")
            f2, s2 = _gradient_optimize(best_f, grad_extra, rng, "final_grad")
            if s2 < best_score:
                best_score = s2
                best_f = f2.copy()
                best_c = _autocorr(best_f)

            extra2 = max(0.0, total_deadline - time.monotonic() - 15.0)
            if extra2 > 40.0:
                bf, bs, bc = _coordinate_polish(best_f, _autocorr(best_f), min(extra2, 180.0), rng)
                if bs < best_score:
                    best_score = bs
                    best_f = bf.copy()
                    best_c = bc

    except Exception as exc:
        print("exception during optimization:", repr(exc))

    # Final safety re-evaluation.
    best_f = _normalize(best_f)
    best_f = np.nan_to_num(best_f, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.clip(best_f, 0.0, 1000.0, out=best_f)
    best_f = best_f / max(1e-300, float(best_f.sum()))
    best_c = _autocorr(best_f)
    best_score = float(2.0 * best_f.size * best_c.max())

    # Optional official evaluation, ignored if unavailable or invalid.
    try:
        out = [float(x) for x in best_f]
        official = evaluate_sequence(out)
        if np.isfinite(official) and official < best_score + 1e-12:
            best_score = float(official)
    except Exception:
        pass

    out = [float(x) for x in best_f]
    print(f"\nFINAL internal score = {best_score:.6f}")
    print(f"FINAL target = {TARGET_SCORE}")
    return out
```
